## Read-outs against the plan (`07`)

| rule | result |
|---|---|
| R1 stable | passes in 11 of 24 equation × degree × library settings, but trivially: almost every group is kept at both radii |
| **R2 readable** | **fails everywhere**: 54-57 of 57 groups kept at degree 2, 174-232 at degree 3 (≤ 8 asked); even at threshold 0.1, 48-52 of 57. Val rollouts blow up in 2-22% of start points (< 1% asked) |
| **R3 predicts** | **fails for every degree and library**. Reach error is lower than frozen at 1-2 s (by 0.05-0.29 units, up to 14%), but at 4 s it ties or is worse; margin error improves at 2 s in some settings only |
| R4 kite | frozen's Blue reach bias in kite windows at 2 s: +0.30 units. L-pure: +0.19 to +0.25 (does not halve). **L+clo weak form: −0.02 to −0.08 (halves)** |
| R5 closure | in L+clo, clo and clo′ terms are kept (with most other groups) |

Facts beside the rules:
- The weak-form regression rows are fitted with R² 0.26-0.50 at degree 2, and coefficients reach 27 at degree 3:
  correlated polynomial columns cancel with large coefficients, so no group falls below the threshold.
- Mixed margin R² at 4 s: frozen 0.19, the variants 0.22-0.33 (blow-ups included, clipped).
- One-step fits (M-step) keep as many groups as the weak form.

**What follows (by the plan):** neither library passes R1-R3, so this is reported as the answer for reach: with a
generic polynomial library, two-level STLSQ found no sparse reach equation here; the hand-built closure stays.
